- **code_reviewer**: Code quality and standards enforcer. Reviews all code changes for consistency, best practices, performance, security, and adherence to project conventions. CRITICAL validation layer invoked AFTER implementation and testing, BEFORE merge. Prevents destructive changes, ensures structural consistency, validates scope, and maintains code quality. Always invoked as final gate before github_ops creates PR. ([Rico-Vari/talos-bug-automata](https://github.com/Rico-Vari/talos-bug-automata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/06_CODE_REVIEWER.md))
